Second transcription's note: When line 2 is zero (STOP), line 6 is also zero, so no separate guard is needed.

Ruling: refusal representation; project retained. The only references the project's expansion has beyond the second's are scope gates the project carries as refusals on Steps 1 and 2 (scope.schedule1, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, sa.excluded_income), which the transcription reports as gates rather than judging per line; the Step 1 to 5 tests, including the Step 4 birth-date question eic.born_after_1960, appear on both sides. The project's first test (AGI below the largest Step 1 limit) is implied by Step 1, question 1. Stage 13 re-run: the second transcription now applies the joint-return identification rules as the project does (household.ctc_ssn, household.all_tin, household.all_work_ssn; i1040gi p. 17, Before you begin, and p. 40, Step 1, question 2).

Second transcription's note: Covers the STOPs that say to check line 27c: Step 1 question 2, Step 3 question 6, Step 4 questions 3 and 5, and Worksheet A line 2 zero. The nonresident-alien STOP also says to check line 27c, but it is refused through scope.other_income. The filer's own choice not to claim the EIC is not asked.

Ruling: representation and scope; project retained; equivalence not proved. The project's expansion differs only by the same scope gates as eic.credit; the line 27c instructions are written in the second's own structure. The project counts each STOP that says to check line 27c only where the flow chart reaches it, plus Worksheet A line 2 zero; ten Stage 9 fixtures assert it, each predicted by hand. Stage 13 re-run: the second transcription now applies the joint-return identification rules as the project does (household.ctc_ssn, household.all_tin, household.all_work_ssn; i1040gi p. 17, Before you begin, and p. 40, Step 1, question 2).

Second transcription's note: Step 3, question 6 says to check the box on a No answer. Following the definition given for this value, it is limited to married MFS/HOH filers who passed questions 4 and 5 and reached Step 3 after Steps 1 and 2.

Ruling: project retained (owner's ruling on the separated-spouses box). Step 3, question 6 is reached only after Steps 1 and 2 are passed (and the box is checked on its 'No'); the project requires them (eic.reached_step3), which brings in the Step 1 and 2 scope gates; the box serves the EIC's special rule. Stage 13 re-run: the second transcription now applies the joint-return identification rules as the project does (household.ctc_ssn, household.all_tin, household.all_work_ssn; i1040gi p. 17, Before you begin, and p. 40, Step 1, question 2).

Second transcription's note: Supplied as a screening input. When it is true, Step 5 line 1 refuses, because Schedule SE is outside the tool.

Ruling: representation; project retained. A member of the clergy with an amount on Form 1040 line 1z and on Schedule SE files Schedule SE, which is outside the tool through scope.schedule1 (i1040gi p. 43, Clergy; Step 5, question 1); the project reads that gate, the second asks the same fact as a separate input. Within scope (scope.schedule1 false) both are false.

Second transcription's note: The grammar's table_lookup has no children operand. The reading chosen adds a 'children' operand (columns 0, 1, 2, 3 or more). The 'Single, HOH, QSS' column also serves married filing separately (the ★ note), and MFJ uses the joint column. The table starts at $1 (eic.table_start), so amounts below $1 give 0. The starred footnote amounts are part of the table. Amounts at or above the limit cannot reach here because Step 5, question 3 requires earned income below the limit.

Ruling: representation; project retained. Both choose the EIC Table column by the number of qualifying children with valid SSNs; the project adds the guard for an earned income below the table's first row ($1), which has no printed row (ambiguity register amb.2025.eic_below_table). Ruling: equivalent representation; project retained. The same choice (line 2 when line 4 is Yes or line 3 is below the line 5 amount; the smaller of lines 5 and 2 otherwise) written as two guards instead of nested choices.

| 2 | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy |
| 4 | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy |
| 6 | eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | eic.clergy |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  # ---------- Step 5 worksheet (earned income) ----------
  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [eic.clergy]
    note: "Step 5, question 1 and the Clergy and Church employees rules change this line when Schedule SE is filed. That case is refused through eic.clergy. Church employee income and other self-employment are outside the tool through the scope.schedule1 and s2.other screens."
  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Schedule 1, line 8s amounts are outside the tool through scope.schedule1, so this line is zero only after that screen."
  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 3)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)", take: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"}
  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "The tool does not ask about the combat pay election. Any nontaxable combat pay is refused through s8812.combat_pay rather than treated as not elected."
  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["eic.line(Step 5, 3)", "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"]}

  # ---------- Shared helpers: children, filing status, limits ----------
  - id: eic.h_children_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children for the EIC who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}}
  - id: eic.h_children_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC (Step 3, question 1), with or without a valid SSN"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}}
  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing a joint return"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
  - id: eic.h_limit0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$19,104 (no children with valid SSNs)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}
  - id: eic.h_limit0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$26,214 if married filing jointly (no children with valid SSNs)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_limit1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$50,434 (1 child with a valid SSN)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}
  - id: eic.h_limit1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,554 if married filing jointly (1 child with a valid SSN)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_limit2_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,310 (2 children with valid SSNs)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}
  - id: eic.h_limit2_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$64,430 if married filing jointly (2 children with valid SSNs)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}
  - id: eic.h_limit3_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$61,555 (3 or more children with valid SSNs)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}
  - id: eic.h_limit3_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$68,675 if married filing jointly (3 or more children with valid SSNs)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}
  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit for your number of children with valid SSNs and filing status (Step 1, question 1 and Step 5, question 3)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit0_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit0_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit1_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit1_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 2}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit2_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 2}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit2_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 3}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit3_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 3}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit3_joint}

  # ---------- Step 1 ----------
  - id: eic.h_s1q1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: Is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than the limit for your number of children with valid SSNs?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_s1q2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 2: Do you, and your spouse if filing a joint return, have an SSN issued on or before the due date of your 2025 return that allows you to work and is valid for EIC purposes?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [household.all_work_ssn, {const: true}]}
    note: "The text asks for an SSN that is valid for EIC purposes. The tool has no separate EIC-validity input, so the reading chosen is household.all_work_ssn: SSNs valid for employment, issued before the due date, for you and for your spouse if filing jointly."
  - id: eic.h_stop_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 2 answered No after passing question 1 (STOP; check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q1, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q2, {const: false}]}]}

  # ---------- Step 2 ----------
  - id: eic.h_capgain
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040, line 7a; if line 7a is a loss, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [f1040.line7a, eic.h_zero]}
  - id: eic.h_investment
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Add the amounts from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, lines 2a, 2b, 3b and 7a. Investment income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_capgain]}
  - id: eic.h_investment_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$11,950"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.investment_limit}
  - id: eic.h_s2_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 2: investment income is not more than $11,950"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_investment, eic.h_investment_limit]}
    refuses: [sd.other_forms, scope.schedule1, scope.other_line16]
    note: "If investment income is more than $11,950, question 3 sends Form 4797 filers to Pub. 596; Form 4797 is refused through sd.other_forms. Without Form 4797 the answer is STOP, so a No here means no credit. Question 4 (Schedule E, rental of personal property, Form 8814, passive activity) is outside the tool through scope.schedule1 and scope.other_line16."
  - id: eic.h_pass12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 and 2 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q1, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1q2, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s2_ok, {const: true}]}]}
    refuses: [sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income]
    note: "Step 1, question 3 (Form 2555) is refused through sa.excluded_income. Step 1, question 4 (nonresident alien) is refused through scope.other_income."

  # ---------- Step 3 ----------
  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_children_any, {const: 0}]}
  - id: eic.h_married_sep_status
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household (Step 3, question 3; Step 4, question 1)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.married, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_s3_q5_fail
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 5 answered No after Yes to questions 3 and 4 (STOP)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep_status, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_s3_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed with a qualifying child (joint return, or questions 5 and 6 passed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_fail, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_s3_stop_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 6 answered Yes (STOP; check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_fail, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}

  # ---------- Step 4 ----------
  - id: eic.h_age_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: people on the return born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "The person roster holds the spouse only on a joint return, so this counts you, or your spouse if filing jointly. The Pub. 596 direction for a spouse or filer who died in 2025 has no input. The reading chosen is the plain birth-date test."
  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: you, or your spouse if filing a joint return, were at least age 25 but under age 65 at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_age_count, {const: 0}]}
  - id: eic.h_s4_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 passed (filers without a qualifying child)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep_status, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}]}
    refuses: [sa.dependent]
    note: "Step 4, question 6 (can you be claimed as a dependent) is covered by the sa.dependent screen and is refused rather than encoded."
  - id: eic.h_s4_stop_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 3 answered No, or question 5 answered Yes (STOP; check the box on line 27c)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep_status, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}]}

  # ---------- Step 5, question 3 and overall pass ----------
  - id: eic.h_s5_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 3: earned income is less than the limit for your number of qualifying children with valid SSNs"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: ["eic.line(Step 5, 5)", eic.h_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_pass_all
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 through 5 passed; go to Worksheet A"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_pass, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_pass, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s5_ok, {const: true}]}
    note: "Step 5, question 2 (self-employed, clergy or church employee filing Schedule SE, statutory employee) leads to Worksheet B. Those cases are outside the tool through scope.schedule1, s2.other and eic.clergy, so only Worksheet A is encoded. Step 6 (the IRS figures the credit) is not offered."

  # ---------- Worksheet A ----------
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"}
  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status, children: eic.h_children_ssn}
    note: "The grammar's table_lookup has no children operand. The reading chosen adds a 'children' operand (columns 0, 1, 2, 3 or more). The 'Single, HOH, QSS' column also serves married filing separately (the ★ note), and MFJ uses the joint column. The table starts at $1 (eic.table_start), so amounts below $1 give 0. The starred footnote amounts are part of the table. Amounts at or above the limit cannot reach here because Step 5, question 3 requires earned income below the limit."
  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}
  - id: eic.h_ws_threshold0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,620"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}
  - id: eic.h_ws_threshold0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$17,730 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_ws_threshold1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$23,350"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}
  - id: eic.h_ws_threshold1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$30,470 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_ws_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold for your number of qualifying children with valid SSNs and filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_ws_threshold0_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_ws_threshold0_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_ws_threshold1_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children_ssn, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_ws_threshold1_joint}
  - id: eic.h_below_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5: is the amount on line 3 less than the threshold?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_ws_threshold]}
  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status, children: eic.h_children_ssn}
    note: "Line 5 is left blank when line 4 is Yes or line 3 is below the threshold. The lookup is computed unconditionally here and used on line 6 only when the text calls for it. Line 3 is below the limit because Step 1, question 1 passed. Same 'children' operand reading as line 2."
  - id: eic.h_line6_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}
  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6"
    label: "This is your earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line2}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_threshold, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.line2}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_threshold, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_line6_smaller}

  # ---------- Intermediate values used by other forms ----------
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit for Form 1040, line 27a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass_all, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line6}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass_all, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_zero}
    note: "When line 2 is zero (STOP), line 6 is also zero, so no separate guard is needed."
  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_stop_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_stop_27c, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_stop_27c, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass_all, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}]}
    note: "Covers the STOPs that say to check line 27c: Step 1 question 2, Step 3 question 6, Step 4 questions 3 and 5, and Worksheet A line 2 zero. The nonresident-alien STOP also says to check line 27c, but it is refused through scope.other_income. The filer's own choice not to claim the EIC is not asked."
  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pass12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep_status, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_fail, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
    note: "Step 3, question 6 says to check the box on a No answer. Following the definition given for this value, it is limited to married MFS/HOH filers who passed questions 4 and 5 and reached Step 3 after Steps 1 and 2."
  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you a minister, member of a religious order who has not taken a vow of poverty, or a Christian Science practitioner, filing Schedule SE with an amount on line 2 of that schedule that was also reported on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Line 27b instructions and Step 5, question 1 (Clergy)"}
    note: "Supplied as a screening input. When it is true, Step 5 line 1 refuses, because Schedule SE is outside the tool."
```
```
